Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the transfer framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 249. The resulting operator achieves bound O(290/n^1).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to transfer structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 249/290 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in transfer theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 127 and the invariant I(A,B) = 283 mod 5.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to transfer theory, we decompose the problem into 127 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 283. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
